## 1980000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フェルムメッセージ開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "페름 메시지 시작<end>"

## 1980001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございますっ！<line-break>大丈夫ですか？<end>"
EN: "Thank you so much! Are you all right?<end>"
KO: "감사합니다! 괜찮으세요?<end>"

## 1980002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お強いんですね。感心します。<line-break>失礼ですが、お名前は…？ <end>"
EN: "You're very strong. I'm impressed. Forgive me for asking, but what's your name?<end>"
KO: "강하시군요. 감탄했습니다. 실례지만 성함은…?<end>"

## 1980003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…さん…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>… 씨….<end>"

## 1980004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たまに悪酔いされるお客さんがいて、<line-break>困っちゃうんです…。<line-break>　<line-break>でも今日は<value:$28>さんが<line-break>いてくれたお陰で助かりました。<end>"
EN: "Sometimes customers don't handle their drink well, and I never know what to do... But you really helped me out today, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "가끔 술버릇이 나쁜 손님이 있어서 곤란해요…. 그래도 오늘은 <value:$28>님이 계셔서 살았어요.<end>"

## 1980005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私、フェルムって言います。<line-break>いつもこの酒場で働いていますので、<line-break>気が向いたらぜひ立ち寄ってください。<line-break><value:$28>さんのように<line-break>強くて親切な冒険者なら、<line-break>いつでも大歓迎です。<end>"
EN: "I'm Ferme. I work here, so please stop by whenever you feel like it. Strong, kind adventurers like you are always welcome, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "저는 페름이라고 해요. 늘 이 술집에서 일하니까 마음 내키면 꼭 들러 주세요. <value:$28> 씨처럼 강하고 친절한 모험가라면 언제든 대환영이에요.<end>"

## 1980006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらっしゃいませ！<line-break><value:$28>さん！<line-break>　<line-break>うれしい。来てくれたんですね。<end>"
EN: "Welcome, <value:$28>! I'm so glad you came.<end>"
KO: "어서 오세요, <value:$28>님! 기뻐요. 와 주셨군요.<end>"

## 1980007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、そうだ。<value:$28>さん。<line-break>実は私、ちょっと<line-break>新しいお料理の研究をしてて…<line-break>あの、それでよろしければ…。<line-break><value:$28>さんも、<line-break>味見してくださいませんか？<end>"
EN: "Oh, right, <value:$28>. I've actually been experimenting with a new dish, and, well... if you wouldn't mind, would you taste it too, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "아, 맞다. <value:$28> 씨. 사실 저 새 요리를 좀 연구하고 있는데… 괜찮으시면 <value:$28> 씨도 맛을 봐 주시겠어요?<end>"

## 1980008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "遠慮しておく<end>"
EN: "I'll pass.<end>"
KO: "사양한다<end>"

## 1980009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごちそうになろうかな<end>"
EN: "Maybe I'll take you up on that.<end>"
KO: "얻어먹을까.<end>"

## 1980010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか…。残念です。<line-break>少し自信あったんですけど…。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、よかったら<line-break>また今度、食べに来てください。<line-break>私、待ってますから。<end>"
EN: "Oh... That's a shame. I was rather confident about this one. But perhaps you could come and try it another time. I'll be waiting.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요…. 아쉽네요. 조금 자신 있었는데…. 그래도 괜찮다면 다음에 또 먹으러 와 주세요. 기다리고 있을게요.<end>"

## 1980011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます。<line-break>今すぐお持ちしますね！<end>"
EN: "Thank you! I'll bring it right out.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 지금 바로 가져올게요!<end>"

## 1980012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お待たせしました。<line-break>どうぞ、召し上がってください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you for waiting. Here you are―please, dig in.<end>"
KO: "기다리게 했습니다. 자, 드세요.<end>"

## 1980013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ど、どうしたんです？<line-break><value:$28>さん…。<line-break>まさか、お口に合いませんか？<end>"
EN: "W-what's wrong, <value:$28>? Don't tell me it's not to your taste?<end>"
KO: "무, 무슨 일이세요? <value:$28>님…. 혹시 입맛에 안 맞으셨나요?<end>"

## 1980015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかわりっ！<end>"
EN: "Seconds!<end>"
KO: "한 그릇 더!<end>"

## 1980016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ご、ごめんなさい！<line-break>おいしくなかったんですね。<end>"
EN: "I-I'm sorry! It wasn't good, was it?<end>"
KO: "미, 미안해요! 맛이 없었군요.<end>"

## 1980017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "料理の本とか読んで、一生懸命、<line-break>勉強したつもりだったんですけど。<line-break>ホント、すみません！<end>"
EN: "I read cookbooks and studied as hard as I could, but... I'm so sorry!<end>"
KO: "요리책도 읽고 열심히 공부했다고 생각했는데요. 정말 죄송해요!<end>"

## 1980018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかった。腕によりをかけて<line-break>作った甲斐がありました。<line-break>すぐおかわりお持ちしますね！<end>"
EN: "Oh, good! I put so much effort into making it, and it was worth it. I'll bring you another helping right away!<end>"
KO: "다행이에요. 정성을 다해 만든 보람이 있네요. 바로 한 그릇 더 가져올게요!<end>"

## 1980019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんなに食べてもらえてうれしいです。<line-break>次も、もっとおいしい料理を作りますね！<line-break>また、いらしてください。<end>"
EN: "I'm so happy you've eaten this much. Next time, I'll make something even better! Please come again.<end>"
KO: "이렇게 많이 드셔 주셔서 기뻐요. 다음엔 더 맛있는 요리를 만들게요! 또 와 주세요.<end>"

## 1980020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんなにいっぱいおかわりしてくれて。<line-break>…うれしいな。<line-break>よっぽど気に入ってくださったんだ。<line-break>私もちょっと味見しておこうかな…。<end>"
EN: "So many helpings... I'm so happy. You must really like it. Maybe I should have a little taste too...<end>"
KO: "이렇게나 많이 더 드셔 주시다니…. 기쁘네요. 정말 마음에 드셨나 봐요. 저도 조금 맛을 볼까….<end>"

## 1980021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "むぐっ…。<end>"
EN: "Mmph...<end>"
KO: "윽….<end>"

## 1980022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な…なに、コレ…！？<line-break>こんな変な味にした覚えは…。<line-break>………………。<end>"
EN: "W-what is this?! I don't remember making it taste this strange...<end>"
KO: "뭐… 뭐야, 이거…!? 이렇게 이상한 맛으로 만든 기억은…. ……………….<end>"

## 1980023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ！<line-break>もしかして、あの時…。<line-break>　<line-break>塩と間違えてヤンバイ草の粉末を<line-break>入れちゃったんじゃ…。<end>"
EN: "Oh! Wait, back then... Could I have put in powdered Yanbai Herb, thinking it was salt...?<end>"
KO: "아! 혹시 그때…. 소금인 줄 알고 얀바이초 가루를 넣어 버린 건가….<end>"

## 1980024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うう…。まさかこんな失敗作を<line-break><value:$28>さんに<line-break>お出ししてたなんて…。<end>"
EN: "Oh no... I can't believe I served such a disaster to you, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "으으…. 설마 이런 실패작을 <value:$28> 씨에게 내놓고 있었다니….<end>"

## 1980025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…でも<line-break>こんなにもまずい料理を<line-break>あんなにも食べてくれた…。<line-break><value:$28>さんって<line-break>強いだけじゃなくて、<line-break>とっても優しい人なんだ…。<end>"
EN: "But <value:$28> ate so much of it, even though it tasted so awful... Not just strong, but very kind too...<end>"
KO: "…하지만 이렇게 맛없는 요리를 그렇게 많이 먹어줬어… <value:$28> 씨는 강하기만 한 게 아니라 정말 다정한 사람이구나…<end>"

## 1980026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "い、いらっしゃいませ、<line-break><value:$28>さん！<line-break>　<line-break>きょ、今日は<line-break>いいお天気ですね。<end>"
EN: "W-welcome, <value:$28>! L-lovely weather today, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "어, 어서 오세요, <value:$28>님! 오, 오늘은 날씨가 좋네요.<end>"

## 1980027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっと、あの…。<line-break><value:$28>さんは<line-break>どんな冒険をされてきたんですか？<end>"
EN: "Um, well... <value:$28>, what sorts of adventures have you been on?<end>"
KO: "저기, 그…. <value:$28>님은 어떤 모험을 해 오셨나요?<end>"

## 1980028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の神器…？<line-break>それってスゴいものなんですか？<end>"
EN: "A Dark Artifact...? Is it something really amazing?<end>"
KO: "어둠의 신기…? 그게 그렇게 대단한 건가요?<end>"

## 1980029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、いえ。もちろん知ってますよ。<line-break>闇の神器ですよね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, no. Of course I know. A Dark Artifact, right?<end>"
KO: "아, 아니요. 물론 알고 있어요. 어둠의 신기죠.<end>"

## 1980030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えーと…。<line-break>……<line-break>……。<end>"
EN: "Um...<end>"
KO: "음…. …… …….<end>"

## 1980031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうそう！　闇の神器といえば、<line-break>小さいころ、おばあちゃんから<line-break>聞いたことがあるんです。<end>"
EN: "Oh, that's right! Speaking of Dark Artifacts, my grandmother told me a story about one when I was little.<end>"
KO: "맞아요! 어둠의 신기라면 어릴 때 할머니께 들은 적이 있어요.<end>"

## 1980032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　どんな話って？<line-break>えーと、そ、その…。<line-break>あれは確か…。<end>"
EN: "What? What kind of story? Um, w-well... If I remember correctly...<end>"
KO: "네? 무슨 이야기냐고요? 저기, 그게… 그건 분명…<end>"

## 1980033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、そうだ！<line-break>思い出しました！<line-break>伝説の…えと、ふ、フライパンです！<end>"
EN: "Oh, yes! I remember now! It was the legendary... um... f-frying pan!<end>"
KO: "아, 맞다! 생각났어요! 전설의… 어, 프, 프라이팬이에요!<end>"

## 1980034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "と、とと…とにかくそのフライパンは<line-break>恐ろしい闇の神器なんです。<line-break>　<line-break>世界を滅ぼすほどの破壊力を<line-break>秘めているほどの…。<line-break>　<line-break>ひょっとしたら<line-break><value:$28>さんが<line-break>探しているのと同じものかも…。<end>"
EN: "A-anyway, that frying pan is a terrifying Dark Artifact. It holds enough destructive power to wipe out the whole world... It might even be the very one you're looking for, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "그, 그러니까… 아무튼 그 프라이팬은 무서운 어둠의 신기예요. 세계를 멸망시킬 만큼의 파괴력을 품고 있을 정도로…. 어쩌면 <value:$28> 님이 찾고 있는 것과 같은 물건일지도….<end>"

## 1980035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの…。<line-break>もしよろしければ、ですけど<line-break>私も協力させてくださいっ！<line-break>おばあちゃんから聞いた話が、<line-break>もしかすると何か手がかりに<line-break>なることだって…。<end>"
EN: "Um, excuse me... If you wouldn't mind, please let me help! Something my grandmother told me might provide a clue...<end>"
KO: "저, 저기…. 괜찮으시다면 저도 협력하게 해 주세요! 할머니께 들은 이야기가 혹시 뭔가 단서가 될 수도 있으니까요….<end>"

## 1980036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "喜んで<end>"
EN: "Gladly.<end>"
KO: "기꺼이.<end>"

## 1980037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "危険なことに巻き込むわけには…<end>"
EN: "I can't put you in danger...<end>"
KO: "위험한 일에 휘말리게 할 수는….<end>"

## 1980038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当にご一緒していいんですか？<line-break>じゃあ私、お休みをいただけるよう<line-break>マスターに頼んでみますね。<end>"
EN: "You really don't mind me coming with you? Then I'll ask the master for some time off.<end>"
KO: "정말 같이 가도 되는 거예요? 그럼 저, 휴가를 받을 수 있게 마스터에게 부탁해 볼게요.<end>"

## 1980039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おいおい、頼むもなにも<line-break>話は全部聞こえてたよ。<line-break>構わんから行ってきな。<end>"
EN: "Come on, no need to ask. I heard the whole thing. Go on, I don't mind.<end>"
KO: "아니, 부탁이고 뭐고 이야기는 전부 들렸어. 상관없으니 다녀와.<end>"

## 1980040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます、マスター！<end>"
EN: "Thank you, Master!<end>"
KO: "감사합니다, 마스터!<end>"

## 1980041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいってことよ。だいぶ長いこと<line-break>休みなしで働いてもらってたからな。<line-break>たまには外の世界を見てくるといい。<line-break>それにお前さんがいなければ<line-break>あの変な勇者もどきも来ないだろうし。<line-break>しばらくは静かになる。<end>"
EN: "Think nothing of it. You've been working without a break for a good long while. It'll do you good to see the world outside for once. Besides, with you gone, that weird would-be hero probably won't come around either. Things should be quiet here for a while.<end>"
KO: "됐어. 꽤 오래 쉬지 못하고 일했으니까 가끔은 바깥세상을 보고 오는 것도 좋아. 게다가 네가 없으면 그 이상한 용사 흉내쟁이도 안 올 테고, 한동안은 조용하겠지.<end>"

## 1980042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "というわけで、しばらくの間<line-break>お世話になります。<line-break>よろしくお願いします。<end>"
EN: "So, I'll be traveling with you for a while. Thank you for having me.<end>"
KO: "그런 사정으로 잠시 신세를 지겠습니다. 잘 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 1980043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "猫屋敷っていうところで<line-break>呼びだすんですか？<line-break>　<line-break>分かりました。<line-break>じゃあ私待ってますね。<end>"
EN: "You'll summon me at a place called Cat Manor? All right. I'll be waiting, then.<end>"
KO: "고양이 저택이라는 곳에서 부르는 건가요? 알겠습니다. 그럼 기다릴게요.<end>"

## 1980044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ私、準備してきます。<line-break>町を出る時に合流しましょう。<end>"
EN: "Then I'll go and get ready. Let's meet up when you leave town.<end>"
KO: "그럼 저도 준비하고 올게요. 마을을 나갈 때 합류하죠.<end>"

## 1980045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか…。<line-break>お力になりたかったんですけど。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>さんが<line-break>そう言うのなら…。<line-break>　<line-break>あ、でも、気が向いたら<line-break>いつでも声をかけてくださいね。<end>"
EN: "I see... I was hoping I could help. But if you say so, <value:$28>... Still, if you ever feel like it, you can always call on me.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요…. 도움이 되고 싶었는데. <value:$28> 씨가 그렇게 말씀하신다면…. 아, 그래도 마음이 바뀌면 언제든 불러 주세요.<end>"

## 1980046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>から聞いたんだけど<line-break>何かくだらない話を知ってるそうね。<line-break>伝説のフライパンだか、何とか。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> tells me you know some ridiculous story. Something about a Legendary Frying Pan.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>에게 들었는데, 무슨 시시한 이야기를 안다면서? 전설의 프라이팬인가 뭔가.<end>"

## 1980047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "は、はい…。<end>"
EN: "Y-yes...<end>"
KO: "네, 네….<end>"

## 1980048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　伝説のフライパンが、<line-break>どこにあるか…ですか？<end>"
EN: "What? You want to know where the Legendary Frying Pan is...?<end>"
KO: "네? 전설의 프라이팬이 어디 있는지… 말인가요?<end>"

## 1980049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すみません…。<line-break>それが私にもよくわからないんです。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry... I don't really know, either.<end>"
KO: "죄송해요…. 그건 저도 잘 모르겠어요.<end>"

## 1980050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、でも…。<line-break>おばあちゃんはこう言ってました。<line-break>　<line-break>とっても恐ろしい敵が現れて、<line-break>窮地におちいったドワーフとエルフが<line-break>力を合わせて作った魔道器が…その…<line-break>その…伝説のフライパンだって…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, but... my grandmother told me this. When a terrifying enemy appeared, the Dwarves and Elves were driven into a desperate situation, so they joined forces to create a magical artifact. And that artifact was... um... the Legendary Frying Pan...<end>"
KO: "아, 그런데…. 할머니가 이런 말을 했어요. 아주 무서운 적이 나타나 궁지에 몰린 드워프와 엘프가 힘을 합쳐 만든 마도기가… 그… 그… 전설의 프라이팬이라고….<end>"

## 1980051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その伝説のフライパンってやつ？<end>"
EN: "You mean that Legendary Frying Pan?<end>"
KO: "그 전설의 프라이팬이라는 거야?<end>"

## 1980052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そのとおりです…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>だから、伝説のフライパンは<line-break>ドワーフの技術とエルフの魔力によって<line-break>強力無比な破壊力を秘めているとか。<end>"
EN: "Th-that's right... They say the Legendary Frying Pan combines Dwarven craftsmanship with Elven magic, giving it unrivaled destructive power.<end>"
KO: "마, 맞아요…. 그래서 전설의 프라이팬은 드워프의 기술과 엘프의 마력으로 엄청난 파괴력을 품고 있다고 해요.<end>"

## 1980053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "酒場でおどけているリルビーの<line-break>ヘタくそな詩より笑えるわ！<line-break>　<line-break>言うに事欠いて、<line-break>高貴なエルフと下等生物のドワーフが<line-break>協力したですってぇ！？<line-break>古今東西、そんなつまらない作り話は<line-break>聞いたことなくってよー！<end>"
EN: "That's funnier than the dreadful poetry Lilubee spouts while clowning around at the tavern! Of all the absurd things to say, you claim that noble Elves joined forces with those inferior creatures, the Dwarves!? Never in any age or land have I heard such a ridiculous fabrication!<end>"
KO: "술집에서 장난치는 릴비의 서툰 시보다도 웃기는군! 하다하다 고귀한 엘프와 하등생물 드워프가 협력했다고!? 동서고금을 막론하고 그런 시시한 이야기는 들어본 적도 없어!<end>"

## 1980054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "つ、作り話なんかじゃありません。<line-break>本当です。信じてください。<end>"
EN: "I-it isn't made up. It's true. Please believe me.<end>"
KO: "지, 지어낸 이야기가 아니에요. 사실이에요. 믿어 주세요.<end>"

## 1980055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカバカしい！<line-break>そんな話、信じるも何も、<line-break>検討の余地すらないじゃない！<line-break><value:$28>の口から<line-break>びしっと言ってあげなさいよ！<line-break>ほら、早く！<end>"
EN: "Absurd! There's nothing to believe or disbelieve―it's not even worth considering! <value:$28>, give a clear answer! Come on, hurry!<end>"
KO: "말도 안 돼! 그런 이야기는 믿고 말고 할 것도 없이 검토할 가치조차 없잖아! <value:$28>가 딱 잘라 말해줘! 자, 빨리!<end>"

## 1980056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確かにおかしな話だ<end>"
EN: "It certainly sounds strange.<end>"
KO: "확실히 이상한 이야기야<end>"

## 1980057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "探すだけ探してみよう<end>"
EN: "Let's at least try looking for it.<end>"
KO: "찾을 만큼 찾아보자.<end>"

## 1980058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でしょ。当然よね。<line-break>はい、これで決まり。<line-break>低俗な夢物語はこれでおしまーい！<end>"
EN: "See? Of course I'm right. There, it's settled. That's the end of this vulgar little fantasy!<end>"
KO: "그렇지? 당연하지. 자, 이걸로 결정. 저속한 꿈 이야기는 이걸로 끝이야!<end>"

## 1980059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかりました…。<line-break><value:$28>さんが<line-break>そうおっしゃるのなら。<line-break>（よかった…。<line-break>　これでもうウソをつかなくてすむ…。）<line-break>　<line-break>（このまま<value:$28>さんが<line-break>　この話を忘れてくれたら、<line-break>　ずっと一緒に旅をしていられる…。）<end>"
EN: "All right... If that's what you say, <value:$28>. (Thank goodness... Now I won't have to keep lying...) (If <value:$28> will just forget all about it, we can keep traveling together...)<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다…. <value:$28>님이 그렇게 말씀하신다면요. (다행이다…. 이제 더는 거짓말하지 않아도 돼….) (이대로 <value:$28>님이 이 이야기를 잊어준다면 계속 함께 여행할 수 있을 텐데….)<end>"

## 1980060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええっ！？<end>"
EN: "What!?<end>"
KO: "뭐!?<end>"

## 1980061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冗談でしょ、<value:$28>！<line-break>まさか、本気じゃないわよね！？<end>"
EN: "You're joking, <value:$28>! You can't possibly be serious!<end>"
KO: "농담이지, <value:$28>! 설마 진심은 아니지!?<end>"

## 1980062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今度こそ、本当にあきれたわ！<line-break>真偽を見極める目を持っていないから、<line-break>あんたたちは下等生物なのよぉ！<line-break>存在もしない伝説のフライパンを探す？<line-break>雲をつかむような話ってよく言うけど<line-break>まさに、これがそうよ！<end>"
EN: "Now I'm truly appalled! It's because you can't tell truth from falsehood that you're inferior creatures! You want to search for a Legendary Frying Pan that doesn't even exist? What a hopeless wild-goose chase!<end>"
KO: "이번엔 정말 질렸어! 진위를 가려 볼 눈도 없으니까 너희는 하등생물인 거야! 존재하지도 않는 전설의 프라이팬을 찾겠다고? 뜬구름 잡는 이야기라는 말이 딱 이런 거야!<end>"

## 1980063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん…。<line-break>ほ、本当に探すんですか？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... A-are you really going to look for it?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님…. 저, 정말 찾으실 건가요?<end>"

## 1980064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バッカじゃないの！<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>世界中どこを探したって<line-break>そんなくだらない魔道器<line-break>見つかるわけないでしょぉ！<end>"
EN: "How stupid can you be!? You could search the whole world and never find a ridiculous magical artifact like that!<end>"
KO: "바보 아니야! 세상 어디를 뒤져도 그런 시시한 마도기는 나올 리 없잖아!<end>"

## 1980065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうしよう…。伝説のフライパンが、<line-break>ただの出任せだって<line-break>わかってしまったら…。<line-break>もう<value:$28>さんと一緒に<line-break>旅をすることなんてできないわ…。<line-break>　<line-break>流れ星に願い事をすれば<line-break>本当になるって<line-break>おばあちゃん言ってたけど…。<end>"
EN: "What am I going to do...? If it comes out that the Legendary Frying Pan was just something I made up... I won't be able to travel with <value:$28> anymore... Grandmother said wishes made on shooting stars come true, but...<end>"
KO: "어쩌지…. 전설의 프라이팬이 그냥 지어낸 말이었다는 걸 알게 되면…. 이제 <value:$28>씨와 함께 여행할 수도 없게 될 거야…. 별똥별에 소원을 빌면 진짜가 된다고 할머니가 말씀하셨지만….<end>"

## 1980066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "早く流れ星を見つけて<line-break>お願いしなくっちゃ。<line-break>　<line-break>私のウソが<value:$28>さんに<line-break>バレないように…。<end>"
EN: "I have to find a shooting star and make my wish, quickly... I'll wish that <value:$28> never finds out I lied...<end>"
KO: "빨리 별똥별을 찾아서 소원을 빌어야 해. 내 거짓말을 <value:$28>님이 눈치채지 않게….<end>"

## 1980067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ…。<end>"
EN: "Oh...<end>"
KO: "앗….<end>"

## 1980068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だ、大丈夫です。<line-break>一晩中、星空をながめていたから<line-break>ちょっと寝不足ぎみで…。<end>"
EN: "I-I'm fine. I stayed up all night watching the stars, so I'm just a little short on sleep...<end>"
KO: "괘, 괜찮아요. 밤새 별하늘을 보고 있어서 조금 잠이 부족할 뿐이에요….<end>"

## 1980069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。本当に大丈夫ですから。<line-break>心配なさらないでください。<end>"
EN: "Yes, I'm really fine. Please don't worry about me.<end>"
KO: "네. 정말 괜찮으니까 걱정하지 마세요.<end>"

## 1980070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "体調を崩すまで、<line-break>星空を見ていたですって？<line-break>…下等生物ってどこまで愚かなの？<end>"
EN: "You watched the stars until you made yourself ill? Is there no limit to the stupidity of inferior creatures?<end>"
KO: "몸이 망가질 때까지 별하늘을 보고 있었다고? …하등생물은 대체 어디까지 어리석은 거야?<end>"

## 1980071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、<value:$28>さん…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "앗, <value:$28> 씨….<end>"

## 1980072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　ああ、私ですか。<line-break>今、ちょっと星空を<line-break>ながめていたんです。<end>"
EN: "Hm? Oh, me? I was just looking up at the stars.<end>"
KO: "네? 아, 저요? 지금 잠깐 별하늘을 바라보고 있었어요.<end>"

## 1980073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私、知らなかったんです。<line-break>世界がこんなにも広く、<line-break>驚きと楽しさに満ちていたなんて…。<line-break>ロストールの酒場は大好きですけど、<line-break>ずっとあそこで働いていたら、<line-break>　<line-break>こんな世界を知ることもなく、<line-break>人生を終えていたのかもしれませんね。<line-break>　<line-break>ありがとうございます。<line-break><value:$28>さん。<end>"
EN: "I never knew the world was so vast, or so full of wonder and joy... I love the tavern in Rostorl, but if I'd spent my whole life working there, I might have died without ever knowing a world like this existed. Thank you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "저는 몰랐어요. 세상이 이렇게 넓고 놀라움과 즐거움으로 가득하다는 걸…. 로스톨의 술집을 정말 좋아하지만, 계속 거기서 일했다면 이런 세계를 알지도 못한 채 인생을 끝냈을지도 모르겠네요. 고마워요. <value:$28> 씨.<end>"

## 1980074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、ときどき不安になるんです。<line-break>このまま冒険を続けていたら、<line-break>楽しいことよりも、辛いことのほうが<line-break>多くなるのかなって。<line-break><value:$28>さんはどう思います？<end>"
EN: "But sometimes I worry. If we keep adventuring like this, will there eventually be more pain than happiness? What do you think, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "하지만 가끔 불안해져요. 이대로 모험을 계속하면 즐거운 일보다 괴로운 일이 더 많아질까 하고요. <value:$28> 씨는 어떻게 생각하세요?<end>"

## 1980075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからも楽しいことがたくさんあるさ<end>"
EN: "There'll be plenty more good times ahead.<end>"
KO: "앞으로도 즐거운 일이 많이 있을 거야.<end>"

## 1980076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "辛くなったらいつでも言ってくれていい<end>"
EN: "If things ever get hard, you can always talk to me.<end>"
KO: "힘들어지면 언제든 말해도 돼.<end>"

## 1980077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですよね。私もそう思います。<line-break>なんか、とてもすっきりしました。<line-break>これでゆっくり眠れそうです。<line-break>おやすみなさい。<value:$28>さん。<end>"
EN: "You're right. I think so too. I feel so much better now. I think I can sleep soundly tonight. Good night, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그렇죠. 저도 그렇게 생각해요. 왠지 정말 후련해졌어요. 이제 푹 잘 수 있겠네요. 안녕히 주무세요, <value:$28>님.<end>"

## 1980078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっぱり、一日でも長く<line-break><value:$28>さんのそばにいたい。<line-break>　<line-break>せめて、私のウソに気づかれてしまう<line-break>その日までは…。<end>"
EN: "I really do want to stay by your side for as long as I can, <value:$28>. At least until the day you see through my lie...<end>"
KO: "역시 하루라도 더 <value:$28>님 곁에 있고 싶어. 적어도 내 거짓말을 들키는 그날까지는….<end>"

## 1980079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よーし、今日こそ徹夜してでも<line-break>流れ星を見つけなくっちゃ。<line-break>頑張ろーっと。<end>"
EN: "All right! Tonight I'm going to find a shooting star, even if I have to stay up till dawn. I can do this!<end>"
KO: "좋아, 오늘이야말로 밤을 새워서라도 별똥별을 찾아야지. 힘내자.<end>"

## 1980080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "つ、つらくなんかないです！<line-break><value:$28>さんと一緒にいて、<line-break>つらかったことなんて一度もないです！<line-break>だって、私は<value:$28>さんと<line-break>一緒に旅ができるだけでも…。<end>"
EN: "I-it's not hard at all! I've never once had a hard time when I'm with you, <value:$28>! I mean, just being able to travel with you, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "힘들지 않아요! <value:$28> 씨와 함께 있으면서 힘들었던 적은 한 번도 없어요! 전 <value:$28> 씨와 함께 여행할 수 있는 것만으로도….<end>"

## 1980081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ…。ご、ごめんなさい。<line-break>明日も早いのに引き留めてしまって。<line-break>どうぞ、先に休んでください。<line-break>私はもう少し、夜風に当たってから、<line-break>眠ります。おやすみなさい。<end>"
EN: "Oh... I-I'm sorry. I've kept you up when we have an early start tomorrow. Please, go on to bed. I'll stay out in the night air a little longer, then turn in. Good night.<end>"
KO: "앗…. 미, 미안해요. 내일도 일찍 일어나야 하는데 붙잡아 버렸네요. 먼저 쉬세요. 저는 조금 더 밤바람을 쐬고 나서 잘게요. 안녕히 주무세요.<end>"

## 1980082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっぱり、一日でも長く<line-break><value:$28>さんのそばにいたい…。<end>"

KO: "역시 하루라도 더 오래 <value:$28>님 곁에 있고 싶어요….<end>"

## 1980083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よーし、今日こそ徹夜してでも流れ星を見つけなくっちゃ。<line-break>頑張ろーっと。<end>"
EN: "All right! Tonight I'm going to find a shooting star, even if I have to stay up till dawn. I can do this!<end>"
KO: "좋아, 오늘이야말로 밤을 새워서라도 별똥별을 찾아야지. 힘내자.<end>"

## 1980084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？　伝説のフライパンじゃと？<line-break>何じゃ、そりゃ？<end>"
EN: "Hm? The Legendary Frying Pan? What's that supposed to be?<end>"
KO: "응? 전설의 프라이팬이라고? 그게 뭐냐?<end>"

## 1980085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、いいんです。ご存じなければ…。<line-break>私も子供のころにおばあちゃんから<line-break>聞いただけですから。<end>"
EN: "Oh, that's all right, if you don't know. I only heard about it from my grandmother when I was little.<end>"
KO: "아, 괜찮아요. 모르신다면…. 저도 어릴 때 할머니께 들었을 뿐이니까요.<end>"

## 1980086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はいっ？<line-break>あ、あれって…なんですか？<end>"
EN: "What? Wh-what do you mean, “that”?<end>"
KO: "네? 아, 저건… 뭔가요?<end>"

## 1980087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　あれって…。<line-break>なんですか？<end>"
EN: "What? What do you mean, “that”?<end>"
KO: "어? 저건…. 뭔가요?<end>"

## 1980088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それと似たような話なら、<line-break>わしも耳にしたことがある。<line-break>じいさんが語ってくれた昔話だ。<end>"
EN: "I've heard a similar story myself. An old tale my grandfather used to tell me.<end>"
KO: "그와 비슷한 이야기라면 나도 들은 적이 있다. 할아버지가 들려준 옛날이야기지.<end>"

## 1980089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "遥か、いにしえの時代…。<line-break>ドワーフとエルフが同じ災厄に<line-break>見舞われたことがあってな。<line-break>それを打開するために、<line-break>両種族が初めて協力して作った<line-break>魔道器があったらしい。<line-break>もしかすると、それが…。<line-break>伝説のフライパンとやらかも…。<end>"
EN: "Long, long ago, both Dwarves and Elves were struck by the same calamity. They say the two races worked together for the first time to create a magical artifact that would deliver them from it. Perhaps that artifact was... this so-called Legendary Frying Pan.<end>"
KO: "아득한 옛날…. 드워프와 엘프가 같은 재앙을 겪은 적이 있었다네. 그것을 극복하기 위해 두 종족이 처음으로 협력해 만든 마도기가 있었다고 하지. 어쩌면 그것이… 전설의 프라이팬이라는 것일지도….<end>"

## 1980090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええっ！<end>"
EN: "What?!<end>"
KO: "에엣!<end>"

## 1980091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じいさんのホラ話と思っていたが、<line-break>他にも知っている者がいたとなると<line-break>こりゃあ、真実かもしれんぞ。<end>"
EN: "I thought it was just one of Grandfather's tall tales, but if others know it too, it may be true.<end>"
KO: "영감의 허풍인 줄 알았는데 다른 사람도 알고 있다면 이거 진짜일지도 모르겠군.<end>"

## 1980092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの…。もしかして、<line-break>どこにあるのか、ご存じでは？<end>"
EN: "Um... might you know where it is?<end>"
KO: "저기…. 혹시 어디 있는지 알고 계신가요?<end>"

## 1980093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあな。<line-break>わしは、てっきり<line-break>じいさんのホラ話と思うとったでな。<line-break>だが、ロストールにも同じ話が<line-break>伝わっとったとなると<line-break>あながち作り話だとも言い切れん。<line-break>まあ、百年以上も前に聞いた話じゃ。<line-break>わしも詳しいことは覚えとらんが。<end>"
EN: "Who knows? I always took it for one of my grandfather's tall tales. But if the same story has been passed down in Rostorl, perhaps it wasn't entirely made up. Mind you, I heard it over a hundred years ago. I don't remember the details.<end>"
KO: "글쎄. 나는 완전히 할아버지의 허풍인 줄 알았거든. 하지만 로스톨에도 같은 이야기가 전해진다면 꼭 지어낸 이야기라고만 할 수도 없겠지. 뭐, 백 년도 더 전에 들은 이야기라 나도 자세히는 기억나지 않지만.<end>"

## 1980094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、<value:$28>さん…。<line-break>どうしたんですか、こんな夜に。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>... What brings you here this late at night?<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>님…. 이런 밤중에 무슨 일이세요?<end>"

## 1980095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　伝説のフライパンの話ですか？<line-break>えーと、あの、その…。<end>"
EN: "What? The Legendary Frying Pan? Um, well, you see...<end>"
KO: "네? 전설의 프라이팬 이야기인가요? 음, 그게, 저…<end>"

## 1980096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、思い出しました！<line-break>昔、おばあちゃんがこんなことを<line-break>言ってたんです。<end>"
EN: "I-I remember now! My grandmother once told me something about it.<end>"
KO: "아, 생각났어요! 예전에 할머니가 이런 말을 하셨어요.<end>"

## 1980097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "長い間、犬猿の仲だったドワーフと<line-break>エルフの橋渡しをしたのは、<line-break>とても偉い賢者様だったとか。<end>"
EN: "Apparently, a very great sage once served as a bridge between the Dwarves and Elves, who had long been bitterly at odds.<end>"
KO: "오랫동안 앙숙이었던 드워프와 엘프 사이를 이어 준 분은 아주 훌륭한 현자님이었다고 해요.<end>"

## 1980098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ？　賢者様の<line-break>心当たりがあるんですか？<end>"
EN: "What? You have a sage in mind?<end>"
KO: "네? 현자님을 짐작 가는 분이 있나요?<end>"

## 1980099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "猫屋敷に住んでいる、<line-break>オルファウスさん…。<end>"
EN: "Orphaus, who lives at Cat Manor...<end>"
KO: "고양이 저택에 사는 오르파우스 씨….<end>"

## 1980100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスさんも賢者だし、<line-break>何か知っているかも、<line-break>…ってこと…ですか？<end>"
EN: "You mean Orphaus is a sage too, so he might know something...?<end>"
KO: "오르파우스 씨도 현자니까 뭔가 알고 있을지도… 있다는 건가요?<end>"

## 1980101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうですね…。<line-break>確かに可能性があるのなら、<line-break>訪ねてみたほうがいいですよね。<end>"
EN: "Y-yes, I suppose so... If there's even a chance, we should go and see him.<end>"
KO: "그, 그렇네요…. 가능성이 있다면 확실히 찾아가 보는 게 좋겠죠.<end>"

## 1980102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec198-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらっしゃい、<value:$28>。<line-break>今日はどうしたんですか？<end>"
EN: "Welcome, <value:$28>. What brings you here today?<end>"
KO: "어서 와요, <value:$28>. 오늘은 무슨 일이에요?<end>"
